	<p>Partner with a Full-Service Amazon &amp; Walmart Agency</p>
</div>
<div class="fl-module fl-module-rich-text fl-rich-text fl-node-elw7xpj3f4u0" data-node="elw7xpj3f4u0" data-debug="version:2|overridden:0|path:wp-content/plugins/bb-plugin/modules/rich-text/">
	<h1><span style="font-size: 60%">Bellavix Amazon Marketing Agency:</span> <br>We grow brands on marketplaces</h1>
</div>
<div class="fl-module fl-module-rich-text fl-rich-text fl-node-95hawt7rpif1" data-node="95hawt7rpif1" data-debug="version:2|overridden:0|path:wp-content/plugins/bb-plugin/modules/rich-text/">
	<p>Your brand is unique, and so are your challenges. We’re here to craft custom solutions that address your specific needs, ensuring your success on Amazon and Walmart. From Vendor Central and Seller Central to PPC Advertising, DSP, and product launches, our tailored strategies are designed to drive your growth and sales across marketplaces.</p>

	<h3>Amazon Advertising Agency</h3>
</div>
<div class="fl-module fl-module-rich-text fl-rich-text fl-node-bwqtrp2nkidc" data-node="bwqtrp2nkidc" data-debug="version:2|overridden:0|path:wp-content/plugins/bb-plugin/modules/rich-text/">
	<p>Bellavix takes a different approach as an Amazon advertising agency. We operate as a boutique partner, not a volume-driven vendor, giving each brand the attention and strategic depth it deserves. Our team is transparent in how we price, how we communicate, and how we measure success. Instead of checking boxes or executing tasks for the sake of activity, we focus on outcomes that drive meaningful growth. From campaign structure to ongoing optimization, every decision is rooted in performance and accountability. The result is a partnership built on clarity, trust, and a shared commitment to improving efficiency, scaling revenue, and making Amazon a true profit center for your business.</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

	<h3>A Marketplace Advertising Agency</h3>
<h3></h3>
</div>
<div class="fl-module fl-module-rich-text fl-rich-text fl-node-sqjnrmuk6w8v" data-node="sqjnrmuk6w8v" data-debug="version:2|overridden:0|path:wp-content/plugins/bb-plugin/modules/rich-text/">
	<p>As a marketplace advertising agency, Bellavix is built around a clear philosophy: marketplaces are not just channels, they are core growth engines. Our best-in-class marketplace thinking shapes how we approach strategy, prioritization, and execution across Amazon and beyond. We are intentional about who Bellavix is and is not a fit for, partnering with teams that value disciplined growth and measurable outcomes. Clients often come to us after comparing Bellavix vs. other marketplace options, looking for a more strategic and accountable partner. From day one, you will understand what it is like to work with Bellavix, with clear communication, structured processes, and performance alignment - supported by a transparent pricing and engagement model designed for long-term success.</p>
</div>

<script type="speculationrules">
{"prefetch":[{"source":"document","where":{"and":[{"href_matches":"/*"},{"not":{"href_matches":["/wp-*.php","/wp-admin/*","/wp-content/uploads/*","/wp-content/*","/wp-content/plugins/*","/wp-content/themes/astra/*","/*\\?(.+)"]}},{"not":{"selector_matches":"a[rel~=\"nofollow\"]"}},{"not":{"selector_matches":".no-prefetch, .no-prefetch a"}}]},"eagerness":"conservative"}]}

<script>
  // Scroll to anchor + playback control script
  document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', function () {
    const columns = document.querySelectorAll('[class*="scroll-to-anchor"]');

    columns.forEach(function (col) {
      const player = col.querySelector('lottie-player');
      let isPlaying = false;

      // Scroll to anchor
      const match = col.className.match(/scroll-to-(anchor[0-9]+)/);
      if (match) {
        const targetId = match[1];
        const target = document.getElementById(targetId);

        if (target) {
          col.style.cursor = 'pointer';
          col.addEventListener('click', function () {
            target.scrollIntoView({ behavior: 'smooth' });
          });
        }
      }

      // Playback control: skip playback control for 'moveLottie' player
      if (player && player.id !== 'moveLottie') {
        player.addEventListener('ready', () => {
          player.play();
        });

        player.addEventListener('complete', () => {
          isPlaying = false;
        });

        col.addEventListener('mouseenter', () => {
          if (!isPlaying) {
            isPlaying = true;
            player.stop();
            player.play();
          }
        });

        col.addEventListener('mouseleave', () => {
          // Let animation finish naturally on mouse leave
        });
      }
    });
  });
</script>

<script>
  /*
    Robust Lottie loader:
    - Animation-first.
    - Fetch JSON yourself (auto-upgrade http->https).
    - On success: render with lottie-web (canvas) in a new sibling container.
    - On failure: hide player, show fallback image.
    - No HTML changes required.
  */
  (function () {
    if (window.__BV_LottieFailover) return;
    window.__BV_LottieFailover = true;

    function ensureLottie(cb) {
      if (window.lottie) return cb();
      var s = document.createElement('script');
      s.src = 'https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/lottie-web/5.12.2/lottie.min.js';
      s.async = true;
      s.onload = cb;
      s.onerror = function () { console.error('[Lottie] failed to load lottie-web'); cb(); };
      document.head.appendChild(s);
    }

    function resolveJsonUrl(player) {
      var url = player.getAttribute('src') || player.getAttribute('data-lottie-src') || '';
      if (!url) return '';
      try { url = new URL(url, location.href).href; } catch (_) { }
      if (location.protocol === 'https:' && /^http:\/\//i.test(url)) {
        url = url.replace(/^http:/i, 'https:');
      }
      return url;
    }

    function findFallback(player) {
      var sel = player.getAttribute('data-fallback');
      if (sel) {
        var el = document.querySelector(sel);
        if (el) return el;
      }
      if (player.id) {
        var a = document.getElementById(player.id + '-fallback');
        if (a) return a;
        var b = document.getElementById(player.id + 'Fallback');
        if (b) return b;
      }
      var sib = player.nextElementSibling;
      if (sib && sib.tagName === 'IMG' && (sib.classList.contains('lottie-fallback') || sib.hasAttribute('data-lottie-fallback'))) {
        return sib;
      }
      var wrap = player.closest('.ratio-box, .lottie-shell, .lottie-wrap, section, div');
      if (wrap) {
        var el2 = wrap.querySelector('img.lottie-fallback, img[data-lottie-fallback]');
        if (el2) return el2;
      }
      return null;
    }

    function showFallback(player, img) {
      if (player) player.style.display = 'none';
      if (img) img.style.display = 'block';
    }

    function keepAnimation(img) {
      if (img) img.style.display = 'none';
    }

    function bootOne(player) {
      if (player.id === 'moveLottie') return; // Skip moveLottie player here

      var img = findFallback(player);
      if (img) img.style.display = 'none';
      if (player) player.style.display = '';

      var jsonURL = resolveJsonUrl(player);
      if (!jsonURL) { showFallback(player, img); return; }

      fetch(jsonURL, { mode: 'cors', credentials: 'omit' })
        .then(function (res) {
          if (!res.ok) throw new Error('HTTP ' + res.status);
          var ct = res.headers.get('content-type') || '';
          if (!/json|text\/plain|octet-stream/i.test(ct)) {
            console.warn('[Lottie] unusual content-type:', ct);
          }
          return res.json();
        })
        .then(function (animationData) {
          ensureLottie(function () {
            try {
              var container = document.createElement('div');
              container.style.position = 'absolute';
              container.style.inset = '0';
              container.style.width = '100%';
              container.style.height = '100%';
              container.style.zIndex = (getComputedStyle(player).zIndex || '0');
              player.insertAdjacentElement('afterend', container);

              player.style.display = 'none';
              keepAnimation(img);

              var loop = player.hasAttribute('loop') || player.getAttribute('data-lottie-loop') === 'true';
              var autoplay = player.hasAttribute('autoplay') || player.getAttribute('data-lottie-autoplay') === 'true';

              var anim = window.lottie.loadAnimation({
                container: container,
                renderer: 'canvas',
                loop: loop,
                autoplay: autoplay,
                animationData: animationData,
                rendererSettings: {
                  preserveAspectRatio: (player.getAttribute('preserveAspectRatio') || 'xMidYMid slice'),
                  progressiveLoad: true,
                  clearCanvas: true
                }
              });

              anim.addEventListener('data_failed', function () {
                container.style.display = 'none';
                showFallback(player, img);
              });

            } catch (e) {
              console.error('[Lottie] render failed:', e);
              showFallback(player, img);
            }
          });
        })
        .catch(function (err) {
          console.error('[Lottie] JSON fetch failed:', err && err.message ? err.message : err);
          showFallback(player, img);
        });
    }

    function initAll() {
      document.querySelectorAll('lottie-player').forEach(bootOne);
    }

    var domReady = (document.readyState === 'loading')
      ? new Promise(function (r) { document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', r, { once: true }); })
      : Promise.resolve();

    var ceReady = (window.customElements && customElements.whenDefined)
      ? customElements.whenDefined('lottie-player').catch(function () { })
      : Promise.resolve();

    Promise.all([domReady, ceReady]).then(initAll).catch(initAll);
  })();
</script>

<script>
  /*
    Beaver Tabs + Lottie: refresh animations when a panel becomes visible
  */
  (function () {
    function refreshLottiesIn(panel) {
      if (!window.lottie || typeof lottie.getRegisteredAnimations !== 'function') return;
      var anims = lottie.getRegisteredAnimations();
      anims.forEach(function (anim) {
        try {
          var wrapper = anim.wrapper || (anim.renderer && anim.renderer.svgElement && anim.renderer.svgElement.parentNode);
          if (wrapper && panel.contains(wrapper)) {
            // Skip moveLottie animations
            if (wrapper.querySelector('#moveLottie') || wrapper.id === 'moveLottie') return;

            anim.resize();
            anim.play();
          }
        } catch (e) { }
      });
    }

    function onLabelActivated(label) {
      var panelId = label.getAttribute('aria-controls');
      if (!panelId) return;
      setTimeout(function () {
        var panel = document.getElementById(panelId);
        if (panel) refreshLottiesIn(panel);
      }, 60);
    }

    document.addEventListener('click', function (e) {
      var label = e.target.closest('.fl-tabs-label');
      if (label) onLabelActivated(label);
    });

    document.querySelectorAll('.fl-tabs-label').forEach(function (label) {
      label.addEventListener('keydown', function (ev) {
        if (ev.key === 'Enter' || ev.key === ' ') onLabelActivated(label);
      });
    });

    var labelsWrap = document.querySelector('.fl-tabs-labels');
    if (labelsWrap && 'MutationObserver' in window) {
      var mo = new MutationObserver(function (muts) {
        muts.forEach(function (m) {
          if (m.type === 'attributes' && m.attributeName === 'aria-selected' && m.target.getAttribute('aria-selected') === 'true') {
            onLabelActivated(m.target);
          }
        });
      });
      labelsWrap.querySelectorAll('.fl-tabs-label').forEach(function (el) {
        mo.observe(el, { attributes: true });
      });
    }
  })();
</script>

<script>
  /*
    Auto aspect-ratio from Lottie comp (w/h).
    Safe add-on: doesn't modify your loader, only sets wrapper ratio.
    Works for animations created by your loader and by <lottie-player>.
  */
  (function () {
    if (window.__BV_autoRatio) return;
    window.__BV_autoRatio = true;

    function setRatioFromAnim(anim) {
      try {
        var d = anim && anim.animationData;
        var w = d && d.w, h = d && d.h;
        if (!w || !h) return;

        var node = anim.wrapper;
        if (!node) return;

        var host = node.closest('.lottie-wrap, .lottie-box') || node.parentElement;
        if (!host) return;

        host.style.aspectRatio = w + ' / ' + h;

        var spacer = host.querySelector('.ratio-spacer');
        if (spacer) spacer.style.paddingTop = (h / w * 100) + '%';

        if (!(window.CSS && CSS.supports && CSS.supports('aspect-ratio: 1 / 1')) && !spacer) {
          function size() { host.style.height = (host.clientWidth * h / w) + 'px'; }
          size();
          window.addEventListener('resize', size);
        }
      } catch (e) { }
    }

    function applyToAll() {
      if (!window.lottie || typeof lottie.getRegisteredAnimations !== 'function') return;
      lottie.getRegisteredAnimations().forEach(function (anim) {
        // Skip moveLottie animation
        if (anim.wrapper && (anim.wrapper.querySelector('#moveLottie') || anim.wrapper.id === 'moveLottie')) return;

        if (anim.isLoaded) setRatioFromAnim(anim);

        if (anim.addEventListener) {
          anim.addEventListener('data_ready', function () { setRatioFromAnim(anim); });
          anim.addEventListener('DOMLoaded', function () { setRatioFromAnim(anim); });
        }
      });
    }

    function init() {
      if (!window.lottie) { setTimeout(init, 60); return; }
      applyToAll();
    }

    document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', init);

    document.addEventListener('click', function (e) {
      var label = e.target.closest('.fl-tabs-label');
      if (label) setTimeout(applyToAll, 80);
    });

    if ('MutationObserver' in window) {
      var labelsWrap = document.querySelector('.fl-tabs-labels');
      if (labelsWrap) {
        var mo = new MutationObserver(function (muts) {
          muts.forEach(function (m) {
            if (m.type === 'attributes' && m.attributeName === 'aria-selected' && m.target.getAttribute('aria-selected') === 'true') {
              setTimeout(applyToAll, 80);
            }
          });
        });
        labelsWrap.querySelectorAll('.fl-tabs-label').forEach(function (el) {
          mo.observe(el, { attributes: true });
        });
      }
    }
  })();
</script>
